def _proj_sum(y, K):
    lo, hi = min(y)-1, max(y)+1 maybe
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > K: lo=mid else hi=mid
    return np.clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```

QP FISTA code details:
```
def _qp_fista(h0,w,n,dx,pad,mu,iters):
    prep=_prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad,trust_mu=mu)
    x=h0.copy(); y=x.copy(); th=1.0
    step=1.0/(mu + dx*(w.sum()+w[n-1]) + 1e-12)
    # maybe step = min(step, 10.0/(mu+1e-12))? 
    for _ in range(iters):
        hh = irfft(rfft(y,pad)*prep.R, pad).real[:n]
        g = -hh + prep.b + prep.diag*y - mu*h0
        x_new = _project(y - step*g, n*0.5)
        th_new = 0.5*(1.0+math.sqrt(1.0+4.0*th*th))
        y = x_new + ((th-1.0)/th_new)*(x_new - x)
        x = x_new
        th = th_new
        # optionally damping y = 0.5*(y+x) if unstable? 
    return _repair(x,n)
```

def _pair_search(h, n, dx, pad, time_limit, rng, base_steps):
   cur=c5
   best=h.copy()
   for scale in [0.02,0.005,0.001,0.0003,1e-5,3e-6,1e-7]:
      if time>limit break
      for _ in range(iterations): # maybe 2000
         c = _cfft(best,pad); active weights maybe top (alpha=1e4)
         prep = _prep_weight(w...)
         g = grad(best,prep)
         # quantiles
         i = choose from bottom 30% of g; j = top 30%
         if g[i] >= g[j]: maybe flip orientation? We want d sign that reduces. Let transfer d from j to i: change approx (g_i-g_j)*d. If g_i < g_j, d>0. Else if g_j < g_i, transfer from i to j (d positive from i to j). So set src = arg high, dst=arg low, dpos in [0,D].
         D = scale * (0.3+...); dhi=min(D, 1-best[dst], best[src]); dlo=0
         if dhi<1e-8: continue
         # maybe also try opposite small -? 
         d,c2 = line
         if c2 < cur - 1e-13: accept
         # occasional random large move: if rng<0.02: random pair D up to 0.1

But recomputing c/g each pair expensive. Recompute after e.g., 20 accepted/50 no? We can compute gradient batches: For current best, compute weights/grad once, select many pairs, line search, accept improvements, then recompute if improved or every 50. This efficient.

Implement batch:
```
def _gradient_pair_polish(h, lim, rng):
  cur=eval; best=h
  for phase in scales:
    improved_in_phase=0
    for batch in range(while time):
       c=cfft(best); w=softmax(c,alpha=1e5? top); prep; g=grad
       order=np.argsort(g)
       nlow=max(10, n//20), nhigh...
       for trial in range(100):
          dst=order[rng.randint(nlow)]
          src=order[rng.randint(n-nhigh,n-1)]
          if dst==src: continue
          D=scale*...
          dhi = min(D, 1-best[dst], best[src]) 
          if dhi>1e-8:
             d = _best_d(best,dst,src,0,dhi,...)
             if d != 0:
                best2=best.copy(); best2[dst]+=d; best2[src]-=d
                c2=eval
                if c2<cur-...: accept; improved++
       if no improvement in batch: break? 
```

Weights softmax: c counts length m; max maybe integer-ish. Use:
```
def _softmax_c(c, alpha):
   z = alpha*(c - c.max())
   z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 0.0)
   w = np.exp(z)
   s=w.sum()
   if s < 1e-12:
      w[:] = 0; w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
   else:
      w /= s
   return w
```

def _pair_coeffs(h,i,j,n):
  m=2*n-1
  A=np.zeros(m)
  # add for variable q sign s
  # sign_i=+1, sign_j=-1
  q=i; s=1
  # positive M
  cnt = q  # 1..q
  if cnt:
      # Apos first: indices M=1..cnt correspond full n-1+M
      # values (1 - h[q-1:q-1-cnt:-1])
      idx = np.arange(n-1+1, n-1+cnt+1) # M=1..cnt
      # We can avoid arange each pair by preallocate? Use slice assignment with negative order.
      # values order M=1: h[q-1], M=2: h[q-2] ... M=q: h[0]
      vals = 1.0 - h[q-1::-1]
      A[n: n+cnt] += s*vals  because full indices n-1+1 = n, length cnt. Yes positive start index n.
  cnt = n-1-q
  if cnt:
      vals = -h[q+1:q+1+cnt]
      A[n: n+cnt] += s*vals
  # negative M=1..cnt; full index n-1-M
  cnt = n-1-q
  if cnt:
      vals = 1.0 - h[q+1:q+1+cnt]
      # full indices: M=1 -> n-2, M=2 -> n-3, ... M=cnt -> n-1-cnt
      # slice end to start reversed. Use idx = n-2 down to n-1-cnt
      # Can do A[n-1-cnt : n-1 : -1] = vals
      A[n-1-cnt : n : -1]? length cnt: start n-2? For cnt=1,n? n-1-1=n-2, stop n? -? Better compute idx = np.arange(n-2, n-2-cnt, -1)
      A[idx] += s*vals
  cnt=q
  if cnt:
      vals = -h[q-1::-1]
      idx = np.arange(n-2, n-2-cnt, -1)
      A[idx] += s*vals
  # c0
  A[n-1] += s*(1 - 2*h[q])
```

Line search evaluation using Afull and current c:
```
def _pair_c5(c, A, i,j, dist,d,dx):
    base = c + A*d
    m = base.max()
    if dist:
       p1=n-1+dist; p2=n-1-dist
       d2=d*d
       v1=base[p1]+d2; v2=base[p2]+d2
       if v1>m: m=v1
       if v2>m: m=v2
    return m*dx
```
But base allocation length m per eval. We can in line search recompute `c + A*d` each time. Grid 15 evals * m=1100 ->16k, fine. Use `np.max(c + A*d)`; returns temp. Good.

def _qp_polish(h, n, dx, pad, time_limit, rng):
   c = _cfft(h,pad); c0 = len?; best=h; best_c=max(c)*dx
   alphas=[1000,5000,20000,100000,500000,2000000,10000000]
   mus=[0.002,0.01,0.05,0.2,1,5,20]

   cycle=0
   while time < limit-20:
      c5=best_c
      for alpha in alphas:
         w=softmax(c, alpha) # c should be current best? Use c=belrace
         for mu in mus:

            if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                best=h2; best_c=c2; c=_cfft(h2,pad)
      cycle++

Function `run`:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try: par=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy()
  except: par=np.full(512,0.5)
  par=_repair(par,len(par))
  best_h=par; best_c=_c5_direct(par)
  candidates=[par]
  n0=len(par)
  extra=[]
  for mult in [1.15,1.4]:
      N=2*int(round(n0*mult/2))
      if 2<N<1024 and N not in extra: extra.append(_resample(par,N))
  budgets = [0.55,0.30,0.15] normalized to budget_s-60
  for i,hc in enumerate(candidates):
      rem=deadline-time.time()
      if rem<60: break
      lim=min(budgets[i], rem-40)
      if lim<=30: continue
      rh,rc,_=_optimize_candidate(hc,lim,rng)
      if rc<best_c: best_h=rh; best_c=rc
  # final polish if time
  rem=deadline-time.time()
  if rem>30:
      # small pair polish on best with very fine scales, use existing c? recompute
      hbest=best_h; n=len(hbest); dx=2/n; pad=...
      c=_cfft(hbest,pad); c5=c.max()*dx
      hbest,c_,c5=_pair_polish(hbest,c,c5,n,dx,pad,rem-5,rng, fine_only=True)
      cd=_c5_direct(hbest); if cd<best_c: update
  best_h=_repair(best_h,len(best_h))
  best_c=_c5_direct(best_h)
  return (best_h,best_c,len(best_h))
```

Implementation:
```
def _qp_step(h0,w,n,dx,pad,mu,iters):
    prep=_prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad)
    L=mu + dx*(w.sum()+w[n-1]) + 1e-12
    step=1.0/L
    x=h0.copy()
    best_x=x.copy(); best_val=_c5_fft(h0,pad,dx)
    for it in range(iters):
        conv = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x,pad)*prep.R,pad).real[:n]
        g = -conv + prep.b + prep.diag*x - mu*h0
        x = _project(x - step*g, n*0.5)
        if it % 10 == 9 or it == iters-1:
            val = _c5_fft(x,pad,dx)
            if val < best_val - 1e-15:
                best_val=val; best_x=x.copy()
    return _repair(best_x,n), best_val
```
If `mu` very large, step small; ok. If `prep.diag` negative? μ>0.002, -2dxw0 min 0.007, diag could negative if μ<0.007? For μ=0.002, diag negative -0.005; F might not be convex! Need ensure μ ≥ 2dxw0. w0 ≤1, 2dx ~0.007 for n=560. So set mu_min = max(0.02, 4*dx*sum(w). maybe). In configs, μ=0.002 not safe. Use mus [0.05,0.2,1,5,20], min 0.05 >0.007. For n=900 dx=0.0022, min ok. Good. L=μ+... diag positive.
But what about offdiag Hessian with weights sum 1: eigen ≤dx, so μ=0.05 safe. Good.

Implementation of `_prep_weight`:
```
def _prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad,trust_mu=0.0):
    m=2*n-1
    wp = w[n:m]  # length n-1
    wn = w[:n-1][::-1]
    a = np.zeros(n)
    if n>1:
       a[1:] = dx*(wn+wp)
    r = np.zeros(pad)
    if n>1:
       r[1:n] = a[1:]
       r[pad-1:pad-n:-1] = a[1:]? Need length n-1. For off indices d=1..n-1, r[pad-d]=a[d]. This is reversed order. `r[-n:]`? If we do r[pad-1:pad-n:-1] = a[1:]? Example n=3,a[1:]=[a1,a2]; r[pad-1:pad-3:-1] length? start pad-1, stop pad-3 (5 for pad8, stop5? inclusive? range 7,6 length2) = [a1,a2] so r[7]=a1,r[6]=a2 correct. Use `r[pad-1:pad-n:-1] = a[1:]`.
    # b
    pos_cum=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1: pos_cum[1:] = np.cumsum(wp)
    pneg=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1: pneg[1:] = np.cumsum(wn)
    b = dx*(pos_cum + pneg[::-1] + w[n-1])
    R = np.fft.rfft(r,pad)
    diag = trust_mu - 2.0*dx*w[n-1]
    return R,b,diag
```
Check r assignment for positive d: r[d]=a[d]. r[1:n]=a[1:] indices d=1..n-1. good.
`r[pad-1:pad-n:-1]` for n=1? skip. For n=2: r[pad-1:pad-2:-1] length1 -> r[pad-1]=a1.
`pos_cum[1:] = cumsum(wp)` length n-1; for i=1..n-1 pos_cum[i]=sum wp[0:i]. correct.
`pneg[::-1]` length n; for i=0 pneg[n-1], i=n-1 pneg[0]. correct.
Linear b: For positive lag d, contributes to i≥d -> pos_cum. For negative lag M, contributes to i<n-M -> pneg[n-1-i]. We used pneg for wn[d]=weight lag -M. pneg[t]=sum_{M=1}^t wn[M]; for i, t=n-1-i => sum M=1..n-1-i wn, i < n-M? M ≤ n-1-i -> M < n-i? If i=n-M, not included; c_{-M} linear includes indices 0..n-M-1, so i≤n-M-1 => M≤n-1-i. correct.

Gradient no trust:
```
def _grad_weighted(h,w,n,dx,pad):
   R,b,diag = _prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad,0.0)
   conv = irfft(rfft(h,pad)*R,pad).real[:n]
   return -conv + b + diag*h
```

`_pair_coeffs`:
```
def _pair_coeffs(h,i,j,n):
    A=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    for q,s in ((i,1.0),(j,-1.0)):
       cnt = q
       if cnt>0:
          val = 1.0 - h[q-1::-1]
          A[n:n+cnt] += s*val # val length q
          val = -h[q-1::-1] # reuse? q-1 reversed same
          A[n-2:n-2-cnt:-1] += s*val
       cnt = n-1-q
       if cnt>0:
          val = 1.0 - h[q+1:q+1+cnt]
          A[n:n+cnt] += s*var
          val = -h[q+1:q+1+cnt]
          A[n-2:n-2-cnt:-1] += s*val
       A[n-1] += s*(1.0 - 2.0*h[q])
    return A
```
Check negative second uses same `h[q-1::-1]` values length q. Good.
But negative first and second both use ranges that may overlap if both cnt positive; adding to same A negative indices with different values for the same M? For a fixed M, a variable can be both first (M≤n-1-q) and second (M≤q). At M in overlap, c_{-M} includes two terms: as first r=q with neighbor q+M and as second r=q-M with neighbor q. Now pair eval:
```
def _pair_eval(c,A,p1,p2,d,dx):
    base = c + A*d
    m = float(np.max(base))
    d2 = d*d
    c0 = len(c)//2
    # m_other if c0 is max and c0 adjusted down
    if d2 > 0.0 and base[c0] >= m - 1e-14:
       if c0 == 0:
          mo = np.max(base[1:]) if len(base)>1 else -1e300
       elif c0 == len(base)-1:
          mo = np.max(base[:-1])
       else:
          mo = max(np.max(base[:c0]), np.max(base[c0+1:]))
    else:
       mo = m
    v = mo
    if p1 is not None:
       v1 = base[p1] + d2
       if v1 > v: v = v1
       if p2 != p1:
          v2 = base[p2] + d2
          if v2 > v: v = v2
    v0 = base[c0] - 2.0*d2
    if v0 > v: v = v0
    return v*dx
```
If p1 None (i=j) no B positive; c0 adjusts. But we won't call if i=j.
If base length 1? n>=2.
`p1` and `p2` are full indices. For dist maybe p1/p2 can equal? no.
If c0 index equals p1? no dist>0.
If `base[c0]` is max but other max equal to c0? mo computed via slices will find equal m, good.
If `d2=0`, v=mo (m), c0 adjust same, p adjust same; returns c5.

Now let's think about correctness of pair update with c arrays c from `_cfft` order. We maintain c as full order [neg rev?]. Need `_cfft` returns full order as np.correlate. Let's implement `_cfft_full(h,pad)`:
```
def _cfft_full(h,pad):
   n=len(h)
   A=np.fft.rfft(h,pad)
   V=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,pad)
   e=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(V),pad).real
   m=2*n-1
   c=np.empty(m)
   if n>1:
      c[:n-1] = e[n-1:0:-1]
   c[n-1] = e[0]
   if n>1:
      c[n:] = e[pad-1:pad-n:-1]
   return c
```
Check positive slice: `e[pad-1:pad-n:-1]` length? For n=3,pad8, stop5, start7 -> 7,6 length2. Good. For general, length n-1. For n=2, stop pad-2, start pad-1 length1.
Negative slice: `e[n-1:0:-1]` start n-1, stop 0 exclusive -> indices n-1,...,1 length n-1. For n=2, start1, stop0 -> [e1]. Good.
Full c[n-1]=e[0]. Good.

Now pair coeff full order: `n:n+cnt` corresponds positive L=1..cnt index n-1+1..n-1+cnt = n..n+cnt-1. Good. Negative slice `A[n-2:stop:-1]` indices n-2 down to n-1-cnt, corresponding M=1..cnt: full index n-1-M. For M=1 index n-2; M=cnt index n-1-cnt. Good. c0 index n-1. Good.

```
def _qp_polish(h,c,c_count,t_end,...):
  best_h=h; best_c=c_count
  configs = [(5000,0.02),(5000,0.1),(5000,0.5),(20000,0.02),(20000,0.1),(20000,0.5),(20000,2),(100000,0.05),(100000,0.2),(100000,1),(500000,0.2),(500000,1),(1e6,1),(1e6,5)]
  while time.time() < t_end - 10:
     # maybe update c from best_h? c should current
     for alpha,mu in configs:
        if time.time() > t_end-5: break
        w=_softmax_weights(c, alpha)
        iters=120 if mu<0.5 else 60
        h2,val=_qp_step(best_h,w,n,dx,pad,mu,iters)
        if val < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_h=h2; best_c=val; c=_cfft_full(h2,pad)
  return _repair(best_h,n), c, best_c
```

for cycle in range(3):
  improved=False
  for ...:

```

`_pair_search` function outline:

def _pair_search(h,c,c_count,n,dx,pad,t_end,rng):
   m=2*n-1; c0=n-1
   best_h=h.copy(); best_count=c_count
   total_tries=int(min(120000, max(3000, (t_end-time.time())*70)))
   scales=[...]
   # weights for scale? If fine_only maybe use scales[-6:]
   weights_s=[1,1,2,2,3,3,4,4,6,8,10,14]
   wsum=sum...
   trial_counter=0
   for si,scale in enumerate(scales):
      tries=int(total_tries*w_s[si]/wsum)
      if tries<30: tries=30
      # limit by time
      g=None; order=None; trial_mod=0
      for tr in range(tries):
         if time.time() >= t_end - 2: break
         # update gradient every 100 or at start
         if g is None or trial_mod%100==0:
            w=_softmax_weights(c, 100000.0)
            prep=_prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad)
            conv=irfft(rfft(h,pad)*prep.R,pad).real[:n]
            g=-conv+prep.b+prep.diag*h
            order=np.argsort(g)
            trial_mod=0
         trial_mod +=1
         D=scale*(0.2+0.8*rng.random())
         # if D too large relative scale? ok
         if rng.random() < 0.7:
            nl=max(5,n//25); nh=max(5,n//25)
            dst=order[rng.randint(nl)]
            src=order[n-rng.randint(nh)]
         else:
            dst,rng.randint(n); src=rng.randint(n); if same continue/ swap?
            if dst==src: continue
         if g is not None and g[dst] > g[src]: dst,src=src,dst
         if g is not None and g[dst] >= g[src]-1e-15: continue? If g equal, line search may improve due quadratic; allow.
         D = min(D, 1.0-h[dst], h[src])
         if D <= 1e-12: continue
         A=_pair_coeffs(h,dst,src,n)
         dist=abs(dst-src)
         if dist:
            p1=n-1+dist; p2=n-1-dist
         else: p1=p2=None
         d, val = _line_pair(c,A,p1,p2,0.0,D)
         if d > 1e-14 and val < c_count - 1e-12:
             d2=d*d
             h[dst] += d; h[src] -= d
             c = c + A*d
             if p1 is not None:
                c[p1] += d2; c[p2] += d2
             c[c0] -= 2*d2
             c_count = val
             if c_count < best_count - 1e-12:
                best_count = c_count; best_h = h.copy()
         # if drift? optional
         if trial_mod % 200 == 0:
             c2=_cfft_full(h,pad); c2_count=float(c2.max())
             if c2_count < best_count - 1e-12:
                 best_count=c2_count; best_h=h.copy()
             if c2_count > best_count + 1e-8:
                 h=best_h.copy(); c2=_cfft_full(h,pad); c2_count=best_count
             c=c2; c_count=c2_count
             g=None
      # end scale
      # final recompute
   # final recompute best
   c=_cfft_full(best_h,pad); c_count=float(c.max())
   if _c5_direct? not here
   return best_h, c_count
```

def _line_pair(c,A,p1,p2,hi,cur):
    if hi <= 1e-14: return 0.0,float(cur)
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,hi,5)
    vals=np.empty(5); vals[0]=cur
    for k in range(1,5): vals[k]=_pair_value(c,A,p1,p2,xs[k])
    bi=int(np.argmin(vals)); bd=float(xs[bi]); bv=float(vals[bi])
    if bv >= cur - 1e-15: return bd,bv
    if bi==0: a=0.0; b=xs[1]
    elif bi==4: a=xs[3]; b=hi
    else: a=xs[bi-1]; b=xs[bi+1]
    fa=_pair_value(...a); fb=...
    phi=1.618...
    for _ in range(10):
       if b-a <= 1e-16*max(1,hi): break
       d1=b-(b-a)/phi; d2=a+(b-a)/phi
       f1=_eval(d1); f2=_eval(d2)
       if f1 < f2:
          b=d2; fb=f1? Wait if f1 lower, new right d2, keep f2? We need maintain x1,x2 and f1,f2.
```

Now `_pair_value` function can return count max. We need use p1 possibly None. If p1 is None but d !=0, c0 adjustment still. For i=j not used.
```
def _pair_value(c,A,p1,p2,d):
  if d==0: return float(np.max(c)) # but caller uses c_count
  base = c + A*d
  m = float(np.max(base))
  d2=d*d
  c0 = len(c)//2
  if base[c0] >= m - 1e-14:
     if c0 == 0: mo = float(np.max(base[1:])) if len(c)>1 else -1e300
     elif c0 == len(c)-1: mo = float(np.max(base[:-1]))
     else: mo = max(float(np.max(base[:c0])), float(np.max(base[c0+1:])))
  else:
     mo = m
  v=mo
  if p1 is not None:
     v1=base[p1]+d2; if v1>v: v=v1
     if p2 != p1:
        v2=base[p2]+d2; if v2>v: v=v2
  v0=base[c0]-2*d2
  if v0>v: v=v0
  return v
```

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _repair(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0).astype(float).copy()
    target=n*0.5
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12: return h
    for _ in range(80):
       if abs(s-target)<1e-12: break
       scale=target/s if s>0 else 0.0
       h=h*scale
       np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
       s=h.sum()
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0: order=np.argsort(1.0-h)
       else: order=np.argsort(h)
       for idx in order:
          if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
          if diff>0:
             a=min(diff,1.0-h[idx]); h[idx]+=a; diff-=a
          else:
             a=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=a; diff+=a
    return h
```
Potential if s=0 scale=0. But target n/2 >0; if all clipped zero impossible? Could happen. Use if s==0: h[:int(target)]=1 etc. Add:
```
if s <= 1e-300:
   h[:target]=1; return h
```
target integer.

Project:
```
def _project(y,target):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=float)
    lo=float(np.min(y))-1.0; hi=float(np.max(y))+1.0
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
    return np.clip(y-tau,0.0,1.0)
```
If y huge range, 45 enough for 1e-13? double range maybe 10; 45 gives 2^-45 ~3e-14. Good.

def _pad(n):
    m=2*n-1
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p
```

_cfft_full:
```
def _cfft_full(h,pad):
    n=len(h)
    A=np.fft.rfft(h,pad)
    V=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,pad)
    e=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(V),pad).real
    m=2*n-1
    c=np.empty(m,dtype=float)
    if n>1:
       c[:n-1]=e[n-1:0:-1]
    c[n-1]=e[0]
    if n>1:
       c[n:]=e[pad-1:pad-n:-1]
    return c
```
For negative slice `e[n-1:0:-1]` length n-1; if n=1, skip. For positive if n=1, skip.
Could e have tiny imaginary? irfft real. Good.

_count_direct_bound:
```
def _count_direct_bound(h):
    n=len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c))*2.0/n
```
Return bound.

_softmax_weights:
```
def _softmax_weights(c,alpha):
    cm=c-c.max()
    z=np.clip(alpha*cm, -40.0, 0.0)
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       w.fill(0.0); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
       w/=s
    return w
```
If alpha small and c differences negative, many exp. Good.

_prep_weight as above. Need handle n=1? n likely even >1. But robust.
```
def _prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad,trust_mu=0.0):
    a=np.zeros(n,float)
    if n>1:
       wp=w[n:2*n-1]
       wn=w[:n-1][::-1]
       a[1:]=dx*(wn+wp)
       r=np.zeros(pad,float)
       r[1:n]=a[1:]
       r[pad-1:pad-n:-1]=a[1:]
       R=np.fft.rfft(r,pad)
       pos=np.zeros(n,float)
       pos[1:]=np.cumsum(wp)
       pneg=np.zeros(n,float)
       pneg[1:]=np.cumsum(wn)
       b=dx*(pos + pneg[::-1] + w[n-1])
    else:
       r=np.zeros(pad); R=np.fft.rfft(r,pad); b=np.array([dx*w[0]])
    diag=trust_mu - 2.0*dx*w[n-1]
    return R,b,diag
```
For n=1, c only L0; pad? 1? okay.

Grad:
```
def _grad_weighted(h,w,n,dx,pad):
    R,b,diag=_prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad,0.0)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,pad)*R,pad).real[:n]
    return -conv + b + diag*h
```

_qp_step:
```
def _qp_step(h0,w,n,dx,pad,mu,iters):
    R,b,diag=_prep_weight(w,n,dx,pad,mu)
    L=mu + 3.0*dx*(float(w.sum()) + float(w[n-1])) + 1e-12
    step=1.0/L
    x=h0.copy()
    best_x=x.copy()
    best_val=float(np.max(_cfft_full(h0,pad)))
    for it in range(iters):
        conv=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x,pad)*R,pad).real[:n]
        g=-conv + b + diag*x - mu*h0
        x=_project(x - step*g, n*0.5)
        if (it % 10 == 9) or (it == iters-1):
            val=float(np.max(_cfft_full(x,pad)))
            if val < best_val - 1e-14:
                best_val=val; best_x=x.copy()
    return _repair(best_x,n), best_val
```
Potential if `np.max(_cfft_full(h0,pad))` repeated; could pass initial c. But ok. Maybe avoid cfft with h0 each q step by cmax initial? Not critical.
If mu high and step small, x may not change much; val initial. Good.

def _qp_polish(h,c,c_count,t_end,n,dx,pad,rng):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=float(c_count)
    best_c = float(np.max(_cfft_full(best_h,pad)))? Ensure.
    c = _cfft_full(best_h,pad); best_c=float(c.max())
    configs=(...)
    cycle=0
    while time.time() < t_end - 5 and cycle < 4:
       improved=False
       for alpha,mu in configs:
          if time.time() > t_end - 3: break
          w=_softmax_weights(c, alpha)
          iters=140 if mu < 0.2 else (80 if mu<2 else 50)
          h2,val=_qp_step(best_h,w,n,dx,pad,mu,iters)
          if val < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=val; best_h=h2
             c=_cfft_full(best_h,pad)
             improved=True
       cycle+=1
       if not improved: break
    _repair(best_h,n); c=_cfft_full(best_h,pad); best_c=float(c.max())
    return best_h,c,best_c
```

def _pair_coeffs(h,i,j,n):
    A=np.zeros(2*n-1,float)
    if n == 1:
       return A
    c0=n-1
    for q,s in ((i,1.0),(j,-1.0)):
       cnt=q
       if cnt > 0:
          vals = h[q-1:0:-1]  # length q? If q=1, h[0:0:-1] empty! Need use h[q-1::-1]
```
For q=1, h[0:0:-1] empty, should be h[0::-1] length1. Use `h[q-1::-1]`.
```
          vals = h[q-1::-1]
          A[n:n+cnt] += s*(1.0-vals)
          stop = n-2-cnt
          if stop < 0: stop = None
          A[n-2:stop:-1] += s*(-vals)
       cnt = n-1-q
       if cnt > 0:
          vals = h[q+1:q+1+cnt]
          A[n:n+cnt] += s*(1.0-vals)
          stop = n-2-cnt
          if stop < 0: stop = None
          A[n-2:stop:-1] += s*(-vals)
       A[n-1] += s*(1.0 - 2.0*h[q])
    return A
```
Check positive slice A[n:n+cnt] uses same cnt for both first/second ranges; but first cnt=q, second cnt=n-1-q. Good. We assign to same `A[n:...]` for ranges: first L=1..q; second L=1..n-1-q. If both positive, overlapping additions correct.
Negative: first M=1..n-1-q, second M=1..q. We assign both to `A[n-2:stop]` with stop based on cnt. If both positive, overlapping additions correct. Good.
But be careful: In loop, for q, if first cnt>0 we assign negative second using `-vals` where vals=h[q-1::-1] length q. If cnt=n-1-q>0 later assign negative first using vals=h[q+1...] length cnt; overlapping negative indices add. Good.

_pair_value:
```
def _pair_value(c,A,p1,p2,d):
    if d == 0.0:
       return float(np.max(c))
    base = c + A*d
    m = float(np.max(base))
    d2 = d*d
    c0 = len(c)//2
    if base[c0] >= m - 1e-14:
       m1 = len(base)
       if c0 == 0:
          mo = float(np.max(base[1:])) if m1>1 else -1e300
       elif c0 == m1-1:
          mo = float(np.max(base[:-1]))
       else:
          mo = max(float(np.max(base[:c0])), float(np.max(base[c0+1:])))
    else:
       mo = m
    v=mo
    if p1 is not None:
       v1=base[p1]+d2
       if v1>v: v=v1
       if p2 != p1:
          v2=base[p2]+d2
          if v2>v: v=v2
    v0=base[c0]-2.0*d2
    if v0>v: v=v0
    return float(v)
```
If m1=1? not.
If p1 None (not), p2 maybe None. Good.

def _line_pair(c,A,p1,p2,hi,cur):
    if hi <= 1e-14:
       return 0.0, float(cur)
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,hi,5)
    vals=np.empty(5,float)
    vals[0]=float(cur)
    for k in range(1,5):
       vals[k]=_pair_value(c,A,p1,p2,float(xs[k]))
    bi=int(np.argmin(vals))
    bd=float(xs[bi]); bv=float(vals[bi])
    if bv >= cur - 1e-14:
       return bd,bv
    if bi == 0:
       a=0.0; b=float(xs[1])
    elif bi == 4:
       a=float(xs[3]); b=hi
    else:
       a=float(xs[bi-1]); b=float(xs[bi+1])
    fa=_pair_value(c,A,p1,p2,a); fb=...b
    phi=(1.0+5.0**0.5)*0.5

       x2=a+(b-a)/phi
       f1=_pair_value(...x1); f2=...x2
       if f1 < f2:
          b=x2; fb=f1? Wait careful.
```

def _optimize_candidate(h0,budget,rng):
    h=_repair(h0,len(h0)); n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; pad=_pad(n)
    c=_cfft_full(h,pad); c_count=float(c.max())
    t_start=time.time(); t_end=t_start+budget
    if budget > 80:
       qp_time=min(budget*0.4, 180.0)
       if n > 700: qp_time=min(budget*0.35, 120.0)
       h,c,c_count=_qp_polish(h,c,c_count,t_start+qp_time,n,dx,pad,rng)
    rem_time=t_end-time.time()
    if rem_time > 20:
       h,c_count=_pair_search(h,c,c_count,n,dx,pad,t_end-2,rng)
    h=_repair(h,n)
    bound=_count_direct_bound(h)
    return h,bound
```

Resample:
```
def _resample(h_old,N):
    n=len(h_old)
    if N==n: return _repair(h_old,n)
    xo=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    xn=(np.arange(N)+0.5)*(2.0/N)
    v=np.interp(xn,xo,h_old)
    return _repair(v,N)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _repair(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = n * 0.5
    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        h[:] = 0.0
        k = int(target)
        h[:k] = 1.0
        if target - k > 0:
            h[k] = target - k
        return h
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    for _ in range(100):
        if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
            break
        h *= target / s
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
        s = float(h.sum())
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                break
            if diff > 0:
                a = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += a
                diff -= a
            else:
                a = min(-diff, h[idx])
                h[idx] -= a
                diff += a
    return h


def _project(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    return np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0)


def _pad(n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _cfft_full(h, pad):
    n = len(h)
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, pad)
    v = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, pad)
    e = np.fft.irfft(a * np.conj(v), pad).real
    m = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        c[:n - 1] = e[n - 1:0:-1]
    c[n - 1] = e[0]
    if n > 1:
        c[n:] = e[pad - 1:pad - n:-1]
    return c


def _cmax_fft(h, pad):
    return float(np.max(_cfft_full(h, pad)))


def _count_direct_bound(h):
    n = len(h)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)


def _softmax_weights(c, alpha):
    z = np.clip(alpha * (c - float(np.max(c))), -40.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        w.fill(0.0)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w *= 1.0 / s
    return w


def _prep_weight(w, n, dx, pad, trust_mu=0.0):
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        wp = w[n:2 * n - 1]
        wn = w[:n - 1][::-1]
        a[1:] = dx * (wn + wp)
        r = np.zeros(pad, dtype=float)
        r[1:n] = a[1:]
        r[pad - 1:pad - n:-1] = a[1:]
        R = np.fft.rfft(r, pad)
        pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        pos[1:] = np.cumsum(wp)
        pneg = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        pneg[1:] = np.cumsum(wn)
        b = dx * (pos + pneg[::-1] + float(w[n - 1]))
    else:
        r = np.zeros(pad, dtype=float)
        R = np.fft.rfft(r, pad)
        b = np.array([dx * float(w[0])], dtype=float)
    diag = float(trust_mu) - 2.0 * dx * float(w[n - 1])
    return R, b, diag


def _grad_weighted(h, w, n, dx, pad):
    R, b, diag = _prep_weight(w, n, dx, pad, 0.0)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h, pad) * R, pad).real[:n]
    return -conv + b + diag * h


def _qp_step(h0, c0_count, w, n, dx, pad, mu, iters):
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    mu = max(float(mu), 5.0 * dx * (wsum + float(w[n - 1])) + 1e-9)
    R, b, diag = _prep_weight(w, n, dx, pad, mu)
    L = mu + 3.0 * dx * (wsum + float(w[n - 1])) + 1e-12
    step = 1.0 / L
    x = np.array(h0, dtype=float, copy=True)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_val = float(c0_count)
    for it in range(iters):
        conv = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x, pad) * R, pad).real[:n]
        g = -conv + b + diag * x - mu * h0
        x = _project(x - step * g, n * 0.5)
        if (it % 10 == 9) or (it == iters - 1):
            val = _cmax_fft(x, pad)
            if val < best_val - 1e-14:
                best_val = val
                best_x = x.copy()
    return _repair(best_x, n), best_val


def _qp_polish(h, c, c_count, t_end, n, dx, pad, rng):
    best_h = _repair(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    c = _cfft_full(best_h, pad)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    configs = (
        (5000.0, 0.02), (5000.0, 0.08), (5000.0, 0.4),
        (20000.0, 0.02), (20000.0, 0.08), (20000.0, 0.4), (20000.0, 2.0),
        (100000.0, 0.05), (100000.0, 0.2), (100000.0, 1.0),
        (500000.0, 0.2), (500000.0, 1.0),
        (1000000.0, 1.0), (1000000.0, 5.0)
    )
    cycle = 0
    while time.time() < t_end - 8 and cycle < 4:
        improved = False
        for alpha, mu in configs:
            if time.time() > t_end - 4:
                break
            w = _softmax_weights(c, alpha)
            iters = 150 if mu < 0.2 else (90 if mu < 2.0 else 55)
            h2, val = _qp_step(best_h, best_c, w, n, dx, pad, mu, iters)
            if val < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = val
                best_h = h2
                c = _cfft_full(best_h, pad)
                improved = True
        cycle += 1
        if not improved:
            break
    best_h = _repair(best_h, n)
    c = _cfft_full(best_h, pad)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    return best_h, c, best_c


def _pair_coeffs_into(A, h, i, j, n):
    A.fill(0.0)
    if n <= 1:
        return
    c0 = n - 1
    for q, s in ((i, 1.0), (j, -1.0)):
        cnt = int(q)
        if cnt > 0:
            vals = h[q - 1::-1]
            A[n:n + cnt] += s * (1.0 - vals)
            stop = n - 2 - cnt
            if stop < 0:
                stop = None
            A[n - 2:stop:-1] += s * (-vals)
        cnt = int(n - 1 - q)
        if cnt > 0:
            vals = h[q + 1:q + 1 + cnt]
            A[n:n + cnt] += s * (1.0 - vals)
            stop = n - 2 - cnt
            if stop < 0:
                stop = None
            A[n - 2:stop:-1] += s * (-vals)
        A[c0] += s * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[q])


def _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, d):
    if d == 0.0:
        return float(np.max(c))
    base = c + A * d
    m = float(np.max(base))
    d2 = d * d
    c0 = len(c) // 2
    if base[c0] >= m - 1e-14:
        L = len(base)
        if c0 == 0:
            mo = float(np.max(base[1:])) if L > 1 else -1e300
        elif c0 == L - 1:
            mo = float(np.max(base[:-1]))
        else:
            mo = max(float(np.max(base[:c0])), float(np.max(base[c0 + 1:])))
    else:
        mo = m
    v = mo
    if p1 is not None:
        v1 = base[p1] + d2
        if v1 > v:
            v = v1
        if p2 != p1:
            v2 = base[p2] + d2
            if v2 > v:
                v = v2
    v0 = base[c0] - 2.0 * d2
    if v0 > v:
        v = v0
    return float(v)


def _line_pair(c, A, p1, p2, hi, cur):
    if hi <= 1e-14:
        return 0.0, float(cur)
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, hi, 5)
    vals = np.empty(5, dtype=float)
    vals[0] = float(cur)
    for k in range(1, 5):
        vals[k] = _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, float(xs[k]))
    bi = int(np.argmin(vals))
    bd = float(xs[bi])
    bv = float(vals[bi])
    if bv >= float(cur) - 1e-14:
        return bd, bv
    if bi == 0:
        a, b0 = 0.0, float(xs[1])
    elif bi == 4:
        a, b0 = float(xs[3]), float(hi)
    else:
        a, b0 = float(xs[bi - 1]), float(xs[bi + 1])
    phi = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0))
    x1 = b0 - (b0 - a) / phi
    x2 = a + (b0 - a) / phi
    f1 = _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, x1)
    f2 = _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, x2)
    for _ in range(10):
        if b0 - a <= 1e-15 * max(1.0, hi):
            break
        if f1 < f2:
            b0 = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = b0 - (b0 - a) / phi
            f1 = _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, x1)
        else:
            a = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = a + (b0 - a) / phi
            f2 = _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, x2)
        if f1 < bv:
            bv = float(f1)
            bd = float(x1)
        if f2 < bv:
            bv = float(f2)
            bd = float(x2)
    for d in (a, b0, 0.5 * (a + b0)):
        v = _pair_value(c, A, p1, p2, float(d))
        if v < bv:
            bv = v
            bd = float(d)
    return bd, bv


def _pair_search(h, c, c_count, n, dx, pad, t_end, rng, max_scale=0.2):
    best_h = _repair(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    c = _cfft_full(best_h, pad)
    best_count = float(c.max())
    c_count = float(c.max())
    h = best_h.copy()

    base_scales = (0.05, 0.02, 0.008, 0.003, 0.001, 0.0004, 0.0001,
                   0.00003, 0.00001, 0.000003, 0.000001, 0.0000003)
    base_weights = (1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 3, 4, 4, 6, 8, 10, 14)
    scales = []
    weights = []
    for s, w in zip(base_scales, base_weights):
        if s <= max_scale + 1e-15:
            scales.append(min(float(s), float(max_scale)))
            weights.append(float(w))
    if not scales:
        scales.append(max(1e-6, float(max_scale) * 0.1))
        weights.append(8.0)

    rem0 = max(0.0, t_end - time.time())
    total_tries = int(min(90000, max(2000, rem0 * 70.0 * 560.0 / float(max(n, 1)))))
    wsum = sum(weights)
    A = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c0 = n - 1

    for si, scale in enumerate(scales):
        tries = max(40, int(total_tries * weights[si] / wsum))
        g = None
        order = None
        since_grad = 0
        since_rec = 0
        for _ in range(tries):
            if time.time() >= t_end - 2.0:
                return best_h, best_count
            if g is None or since_grad >= 100:
                w = _softmax_weights(c, 100000.0)
                g = _grad_weighted(h, w, n, dx, pad)
                order = np.argsort(g)
                since_grad = 0
            since_grad += 1
            since_rec += 1

            if rng.random() < 0.78:
                nl = max(5, n // 24)
                nh = max(5, n // 24)
                dst = int(order[rng.randint(nl)])
                src = int(order[n - 1 - rng.randint(nh)])
            else:
                dst = int(rng.randint(n))
                src = int(rng.randint(n))
            if dst == src:
                continue
            if g is not None and g[dst] > g[src]:
                dst, src = src, dst
                if dst == src:
                    continue

            D = scale * (0.2 + 0.8 * rng.random())
            D = min(D, 1.0 - h[dst], h[src])
            D = min(D, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[dst] - 1e-12), max(0.0, h[src] - 1e-12))
            if D <= 1e-13:
                continue

            _pair_coeffs_into(A, h, dst, src, n)
            dist = abs(dst - src)
            if dist:
                p1 = n - 1 + dist
                p2 = n - 1 - dist
            else:
                p1 = p2 = None
            d, val = _line_pair(c, A, p1, p2, D, c_count)

            if d > 1e-14 and val < c_count - 1e-11:
                d2 = d * d
                c_old = c
                h[dst] += d
                h[src] -= d
                c = c_old + A * d
                if p1 is not None:
                    c[p1] += d2
                    if p2 != p1:
                        c[p2] += d2
                c[c0] -= 2.0 * d2
                actual = float(c.max())
                if actual < c_count - 1e-13:
                    c_count = actual
                    if c_count < best_count:
                        best_count = c_count
                        best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    h[dst] -= d
                    h[src] += d
                    c = c_old

            if since_rec >= 250:
                c2 = _cfft_full(h, pad)
                c2_count = float(c2.max())
                if c2_count < best_count - 1e-12:
                    best_count = c2_count
                    best_h = h.copy()
                elif c2_count > best_count + 1e-7:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    c2 = _cfft_full(h, pad)
                    c2_count = best_count
                c = c2
                c_count = c2_count
                g = None
                order = None
                since_grad = 0
                since_rec = 0

    best_h = _repair(best_h, n)
    c = _cfft_full(best_h, pad)
    best_count = float(c.max())
    return best_h, best_count


def _resample(h_old, N):
    n = len(h_old)
    if N == n:
        return _repair(h_old, n)
    xo = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    xn = (np.arange(N) + 0.5) * (2.0 / N)
    v = np.interp(xn, xo, h_old)
    return _repair(v, N)


def _optimize_candidate(h0, budget, rng):
    h = _repair(np.array(h0, dtype=float, copy=True), len(h0))
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    pad = _pad(n)
    c = _cfft_full(h, pad)
    c_count = float(c.max())

    t_end = time.time() + float(budget)
    if budget > 80 and time.time() < t_end - 30:
        qp_time = min(budget * 0.42, 180.0)
        if n > 720:
            qp_time = min(budget * 0.36, 130.0)
        h, c, c_count = _qp_polish(h, c, c_count, time.time() + qp_time, n, dx, pad, rng)

    rem = t_end - time.time()
    if rem > 25:
        h, c_count = _pair_search(h, c, c_count, n, dx, pad, t_end - 2.0, rng, max_scale=0.05)

    h = _repair(h, n)
    return h, _count_direct_bound(h)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        par = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        par = np.full(512, 0.5, dtype=float)
    par = _repair(par, len(par))

    best_h = par.copy()
    best_bound = _count_direct_bound(par)

    n0 = len(par)
    cands = [par]
    for mult in (1.15, 1.45):
        N = 2 * int(round(n0 * mult / 2.0))
        if N > max(4, n0 + 20) and N < 1024:
            cands.append(_resample(par, N))

    usable = max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 70.0)
    if len(cands) == 1:
        lim = usable - 20.0
    else:
        per = [0.55, 0.28, 0.17] + [0.10] * 5
        for pi, hc in enumerate(cands):
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 70:
                break
            if pi == len(cands) - 1:
                lim = max(20.0, rem - 25.0)
            else:
                lim = min(rem - 70 * (len(cands) - pi - 1), usable * per[pi % len(per)])
            if lim < 25:
                continue
            h, b = _optimize_candidate(hc, lim, rng)
            if b < best_bound:
                best_bound = b
                best_h = h

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 45:
        h = _repair(best_h.copy(), len(best_h))
        n = len(h)
        dx = 2.0 / n
        pad = _pad(n)
        c = _cfft_full(h, pad)
        h, cc = _pair_search(h, c, float(c.max()), n, dx, pad, deadline - 3.0, rng, max_scale=1e-5)
        h = _repair(h, n)
        b = _count_direct_bound(h)
        if b < best_bound:
            best_bound = b
            best_h = h

    best_h = _repair(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_bound = _count_direct_bound(best_h)
    return (best_h, float(best_bound), len(best_h))
```
